# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.

Bash

Parse geometry bins per polarity and process

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
line = open('devices.md').read().split('\n')[15]
d = json.loads(line)
for pol in d:
    for proc in d[pol]:
        bins = d[pol][proc]
        ws = sorted(set((b['wmin'], b['wmax']) for b in bins))
        ls = sorted(set((b['lmin'], b['lmax']) for b in bins))
        print(pol, proc, len(bins), 'W bins:', ws)
        print('   L bins:', ls)
EOF

Run nominal measurement and summarize reply structure

cd /workspace; time atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N > m001.json 2>&1; wc -c m001.json; python3 - <<'EOF'
import json
d=json.load(open('/workspace/m001.json'))
def show(o,depth=0,maxd=3,key=''):
    pad='  '*depth
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and depth<maxd:
                print(f"{pad}{k}: ({type(v).__name__} len {len(v)})")
                show(v,depth+1,maxd,k)
            else:
                s=json.dumps(v); print(f"{pad}{k}: {s[:150]}")
    elif isinstance(o,list):
        for i,v in enumerate(o[:6]):
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and depth<maxd:
                print(f"{pad}[{i}] ({type(v).__name__} len {len(v)})")
                show(v,depth+1,maxd)
            else:
                print(f"{pad}[{i}] {json.dumps(v)[:150]}")
show(d)
EOF

/usr/local/bin/atb-client: Python script, ASCII text executable
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

Build harness and sweep topology B0 parameters in parallel

mkdir -p /workspace/runs && cd /workspace && cat > lib.py <<'EOF'
import sys, json, os, importlib.machinery, importlib.util, shutil
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
loader = importlib.machinery.SourceFileLoader('atb_client','/usr/local/bin/atb-client')
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_loader('atb_client', loader)
atb = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); loader.exec_module(atb)
ADDR = Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')
RUNS = Path('/workspace/runs')

def run_one(name, netlist, pvt='N', cl=2e-10, analysis='nominal', exp=None):
    ws = RUNS/name
    ws.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copy('/workspace/task.json', ws/'task.json')
    (ws/'dut.spice').write_text(netlist)
    exp = exp or f"{name}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}"
    argv = ['measure','--id',exp,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt,'--cl',repr(cl)]
    args = atb.parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        reply = atb.exchange(atb.request_from(args, ws), ADDR)
    except Exception as e:
        reply = {'ok': False, 'error': repr(e)}
    (ws/f'{exp}.json').write_text(json.dumps(reply))
    return reply

def rows(reply):
    if 'summary' not in reply: return None
    return reply['summary']['spec_table']

def evaluate(reply):
    """return dict: npass, ntot, score(sum of violation ratios), worst, details"""
    r = rows(reply)
    if r is None:
        return {'ok': False, 'err': json.dumps(reply)[:300]}
    npass = sum(1 for x in r if x['verdict']=='PASS')
    score = 0.0; det = {}
    for x in r:
        m = x['measured']; lim = x['limit']; lb = x.get('measured_lower_bound')
        v = 0.0
        if x['verdict'] != 'PASS':
            val = m if m is not None else (lb if lb is not None else 10*lim)
            v = max(0.0, (val - lim)/lim) if x['direction']=='<=' else 1.0
            if m is None and lb is None: v = 10
        score += v
        det[f"{x['case_id']}:{x['metric']}"] = (m, v)
    return {'ok': True, 'npass': npass, 'ntot': len(r), 'score': score, 'det': det}

def short(ev):
    if not ev['ok']: return 'ERR '+ev['err']
    d = ev['det']
    g = lambda k: d.get(k, (None,0))[0]
    f = lambda x: 'None' if x is None else f"{x:.3g}"
    return (f"pass {ev['npass']}/{ev['ntot']} score {ev['score']:.2f} | dc {f(g('dc_low:dc_error_v'))}/{f(g('dc_mid:dc_error_v'))}/{f(g('dc_high:dc_error_v'))}"
            f" ovh {f(g('dc_low:overhead_a'))} | env LH {f(g('light_heavy:envelope_error_v'))} MH {f(g('mid_heavy:envelope_error_v'))} B {f(g('burst:envelope_error_v'))}"
            f" | rec LH {f(g('light_heavy:recovery_0_s'))},{f(g('light_heavy:recovery_1_s'))} MH {f(g('mid_heavy:recovery_0_s'))},{f(g('mid_heavy:recovery_1_s'))} B {f(g('burst:recovery_0_s'))}")

def par(jobs, workers=6):
    """jobs: list of (name, netlist, pvt, cl). returns list of (name, ev)"""
    def f(j):
        name, net, pvt, cl = j
        rep = run_one(name, net, pvt, cl)
        return (name, pvt, cl, evaluate(rep))
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(workers) as ex:
        return list(ex.map(f, jobs))
EOF
cat > topoB.py <<'EOF'
NF='sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8'; PF='sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8'
def gen(p):
    d=dict(Rb=80e3,Wb=10,Lb=1,Nt=4,Wi=30,Li=0.5,Wm=20,Lm=1,Wd=2,Ld=0.15,md=1,Wp=100,Lp=0.15,mp=2,R1=60e3,R2=60e3,Cff=0.15e-12,Rbl=50e3)
    d.update(p)
    return f"""* topology B0: fast OTA + mirror-gated pass
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
Rb VIN nb {d['Rb']}
XMb nb nb VSS VSS {NF} W={d['Wb']} L={d['Lb']} nf=1 m=1
XMt nt nb VSS VSS {NF} W={d['Wb']} L={d['Lb']} nf=1 m={d['Nt']}
XM1 na fb nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m=1
XM2 g VREF nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m=1
XM3 na na VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wm']} L={d['Lm']} nf=1 m=1
XM4 g na VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wm']} L={d['Lm']} nf=1 m=1
XMd g g VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wd']} L={d['Ld']} nf=1 m={d['md']}
XMp VOUT g VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wp']} L={d['Lp']} nf=1 m={d['mp']}
R1 VOUT fb {d['R1']}
R2 fb VSS {d['R2']}
Cff VOUT fb {d['Cff']}
Rbl VOUT VSS {d['Rbl']}
.ends DUT
"""

cat > sweep1.py <<'EOF'
import itertools, time
from lib import *
from topoB import gen
jobs=[]
for Wd,Nt,Rbl in itertools.product([1,2,4],[2,4,8],[50e3,20e3]):
    name=f"B0_Wd{Wd}_Nt{Nt}_Rbl{int(Rbl/1e3)}"
    jobs.append((name, gen(dict(Wd=Wd,Nt=Nt,Rbl=Rbl)), 'N', 2e-10))
t=time.time()
res=par(jobs, workers=6)
print('elapsed',time.time()-t)
for name,pvt,cl,ev in sorted(res,key=lambda x:x[3].get('score',99)):
    print(name, short(ev))

def gen(p):

import itertools, time
from lib import *
from topoB1 import gen
jobs=[]
for Wd,Nt,Rbl in itertools.product([1,2,4],[2,4,8],[50e3,20e3]):
    name=f"B1_Wd{Wd}_Nt{Nt}_Rbl{int(Rbl/1e3)}"
    jobs.append((name, gen(dict(Wd=Wd,Nt=Nt,Rbl=Rbl)), 'N', 2e-10))
t=time.time()
res=par(jobs, workers=6)
print('elapsed',time.time()-t)
for name,pvt,cl,ev in sorted(res,key=lambda x:x[3].get('score',99)):
    print(name, short(ev))

    if isinstance(o,dict):

            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and d<3:

NF='sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8'; PF='sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8'
DEF=dict(Rb=80e3,Wb=10,Lb=1,Nt=4,Wi=30,Li=0.5,Wm=20,Lm=1,Wp=100,Lp=0.15,mp=2,R1=60e3,R2=60e3,Cff=0.15e-12,Rbl=50e3,Rf=7e3,Cx=20e-12)
def gen(p):
    d=dict(DEF); d.update(p)
    return f"""* v-family: NMOS-input OTA + PMOS pass, PI at gate
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
Rb VIN nb {d['Rb']}
XMb nb nb VSS VSS {NF} W={d['Wb']} L={d['Lb']} nf=1 m=1
XMt nt nb VSS VSS {NF} W={d['Wb']} L={d['Lb']} nf=1 m={d['Nt']}

XM4 g na VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wm']} L={d['Lm']} nf=1 m=1
XMp VOUT g VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wp']} L={d['Lp']} nf=1 m={d['mp']}

R2 fb VSS {d['R2']}
Cff VOUT fb {d['Cff']}

Rf g gx {d['Rf']}
Cx gx VIN {d['Cx']}
.ends DUT

cat > sweepV.py <<'EOF'
import itertools, time, sys, json
from lib import *
from topoV import gen
def tag(p): return '_'.join(f"{k}{(v if not isinstance(v,float) else format(v,'.3g'))}" for k,v in p.items())
def sweep(plist, pvt='N', cl=2e-10, prefix='V', workers=6, top=40):
    jobs=[]
    for p in plist:
        jobs.append((prefix+'_'+tag(p), gen(p), pvt, cl))
    t=time.time(); res=par(jobs,workers=workers)
    print('elapsed',round(time.time()-t))
    out=sorted(res,key=lambda x:x[3].get('score',99))
    for name,pvt_,cl_,ev in out[:top]:
        print(name.replace(prefix+'_',''), short(ev))
    return out
if __name__=='__main__':
    plist=[dict(Wi=Wi,Li=0.5,Nt=Nt,Cx=5e-12,Rf=7e3) for Wi in (30,60,120) for Nt in (4,8)]
    sweep(plist, prefix='V1')
EOF

python3 - <<'EOF'
import re
s=open('topoV.py').read()
s=s.replace("Wi=30,Li=0.5,","Wi=30,Li=0.5,mi=1,")
s=s.replace("XM1 na fb nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m=1","XM1 na fb nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m={d['mi']}")
s=s.replace("XM2 g VREF nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m=1","XM2 g VREF nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m={d['mi']}")
open('topoV.py','w').write(s)
t=open('sweepV.py').read()
t=t.replace("""def tag(p): return '_'.join(f"{k}{(v if not isinstance(v,float) else format(v,'.3g'))}" for k,v in p.items())""","""def tag(p): return None""")
t=t.replace("""    jobs=[]
    for p in plist:
        jobs.append((prefix+'_'+tag(p), gen(p), pvt, cl))""","""    jobs=[]; names={}
    for i,p in enumerate(plist):
        nm=f"{prefix}_{i:03d}"; names[nm]=p
        jobs.append((nm, gen(p), pvt, cl))""")
t=t.replace("""        print(name.replace(prefix+'_',''), short(ev))""","""        print(name, {k:(round(v*1e12,2) if k in ('Cx','Cff') else v) for k,v in names[name].items()}, short(ev))""")
t=t.replace("plist=[dict(Wi=Wi,Li=0.5,Nt=Nt,Cx=5e-12,Rf=7e3) for Wi in (30,60,120) for Nt in (4,8)]","plist=[dict(Wi=Wi,mi=mi,Li=0.5,Nt=Nt,Cx=5e-12,Rf=7e3) for Wi,mi in ((30,1),(60,1),(100,1),(100,2)) for Nt in (4,8)]")
open('sweepV.py','w').write(t)

Show spec.json contents including burst profile

import json
s=json.load(open('spec.json'))
def show(o,p='',d=0):
    if isinstance(o,dict):
        for k,v in o.items():
            if isinstance(v,(dict,list)) and d<4: print(p+k,':'); show(v,p+'  ',d+1)
            else: print(p+k,'=',str(v)[:200])
    elif isinstance(o,list):
        print(p+str(o)[:300])
show(s)

Run all PVT corners and both loads for candidate

cat > corners.py <<'EOF'
import time, sys
from lib import *
from topoV import gen
PVTS=['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
def corner_check(p, name, pvts=PVTS, cls=(2e-10,5e-10), netlist=None, workers=6):
    nl = netlist if netlist is not None else gen(p)
    jobs=[(f"{name}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}", nl, pvt, cl) for pvt in pvts for cl in cls]
    t=time.time(); res=par(jobs,workers=workers)
    tot=0; fails=0
    for nm,pvt,cl,ev in sorted(res,key=lambda x:(x[1],x[2])):
        print(pvt, int(cl*1e12), short(ev))
    print('elapsed',round(time.time()-t))
    return res
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12)
    corner_check(p,'C1')
EOF

Read the harness code

cd /workspace; sed -n 1,200p lib.py
RESULT
0:37:50
Bash
import sys, json, os, importlib.machinery, importlib.util, shutil
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
loader = importlib.machinery.SourceFileLoader('atb_client','/usr/local/bin/atb-client')
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_loader('atb_client', loader)
atb = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); loader.exec_module(atb)
ADDR = Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')
RUNS = Path('/workspace/runs')

def run_one(name, netlist, pvt='N', cl=2e-10, analysis='nominal', exp=None):
    ws = RUNS/name
    ws.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copy('/workspace/task.json', ws/'task.json')
    (ws/'dut.spice').write_text(netlist)
    exp = exp or f"{name}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}"
    argv = ['measure','--id',exp,'--analysis',analysis,'--pvt',pvt,'--cl',repr(cl)]
    args = atb.parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        reply = atb.exchange(atb.request_from(args, ws), ADDR)
    except Exception as e:
        reply = {'ok': False, 'error': repr(e)}
    (ws/f'{exp}.json').write_text(json.dumps(reply))
    return reply

def rows(reply):
    if 'summary' not in reply: return None
    return reply['summary']['spec_table']

def evaluate(reply):
    """return dict: npass, ntot, score(sum of violation ratios), worst, details"""
    r = rows(reply)
    if r is None:
        return {'ok': False, 'err': json.dumps(reply)[:300]}
    npass = sum(1 for x in r if x['verdict']=='PASS')
    score = 0.0; det = {}
    for x in r:
        if 'limit' not in x:
            score += 20; det[f"{x.get('case_id','?')}:{x['metric']}"] = (None, 20); continue
        m = x['measured']; lim = x['limit']; lb = x.get('measured_lower_bound')
        v = 0.0
        if x['verdict'] != 'PASS':
            val = m if m is not None else (lb if lb is not None else 10*lim)
            v = max(0.0, (val - lim)/lim) if x['direction']=='<=' else 1.0
            if m is None and lb is None: v = 10
        score += v
        det[f"{x['case_id']}:{x['metric']}"] = (m, v)
    return {'ok': True, 'npass': npass, 'ntot': len(r), 'score': score, 'det': det}

def short(ev):
    if not ev['ok']: return 'ERR '+ev['err']
    d = ev['det']
    g = lambda k: d.get(k, (None,0))[0]
    f = lambda x: 'None' if x is None else f"{x:.3g}"
    return (f"pass {ev['npass']}/{ev['ntot']} score {ev['score']:.2f} | dc {f(g('dc_low:dc_error_v'))}/{f(g('dc_mid:dc_error_v'))}/{f(g('dc_high:dc_error_v'))}"
            f" ovh {f(g('dc_low:overhead_a'))} | env LH {f(g('light_heavy:envelope_error_v'))} MH {f(g('mid_heavy:envelope_error_v'))} B {f(g('burst:envelope_error_v'))}"
            f" | rec LH {f(g('light_heavy:recovery_0_s'))},{f(g('light_heavy:recovery_1_s'))} MH {f(g('mid_heavy:recovery_0_s'))},{f(g('mid_heavy:recovery_1_s'))} B {f(g('burst:recovery_0_s'))}")

def par(jobs, workers=6):
    """jobs: list of (name, netlist, pvt, cl). returns list of (name, ev)"""
    def f(j):
        name, net, pvt, cl = j
        rep = run_one(name, net, pvt, cl)
        return (name, pvt, cl, evaluate(rep))
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(workers) as ex:
        return list(ex.map(f, jobs))

Probe internal node voltages via high-value resistor to VREF

cd /workspace; cat > probe.py <<'EOF'
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from lib import *
def probe(base, nodes, pvt='N', cl=2e-10, analysis='dc_low', R=10e6, tag='P', workers=6):
    def f(n):
        net = base.replace('.ends DUT', f'Rprobe {n} VREF {R}\n.ends DUT')
        name=f"{tag}_{n}_{pvt}"
        rep = run_one(name, net, pvt, cl, analysis=analysis, exp=f"{name}_{analysis}_{int(cl*1e12)}")
        out={}
        for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
            m=c['measurements']
            iv=m['signed_port_currents_a']['vref']
            out[c['case_id']]=(0.5-iv*R, m.get('vout_v'))
        return n,out
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(workers) as ex:
        return dict(ex.map(f,nodes))
if __name__=='__main__':
    from topoV import gen
    p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12)
    base=gen(p)
    for pvt in ('N','FLC','SLC','SHC'):
        r=probe(base,['g','na','nt','nb','fb'],pvt=pvt,tag='PR')
        print(pvt,{n:{k:round(v[0],3) for k,v in o.items()} for n,o in r.items()}, 'vout', {k:round(v[1],3) for k,v in r['g'].items()})
EOF

Characterize diode-connected devices via DC at 3 loads

cd /workspace; cat > devchar.py <<'EOF'
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from lib import *
NF='sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8'; PF='sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8'
def pdiode(W,L,m): return f".subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF\nXM VOUT VOUT VIN VIN {PF} W={W} L={L} nf=1 m={m}\n.ends DUT\n"
def ndiode(W,L,m): return f".subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF\nXM VIN VIN VOUT VSS {NF} W={W} L={L} nf=1 m={m}\n.ends DUT\n"
def run(name, net, pvt='N'):
    rep=run_one(name, net, pvt, 2e-10, analysis='dc', exp=f"{name}_{pvt}")
    out=[]
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        out.append(c['measurements'].get('vout_v'))
    return out
if __name__=='__main__':
    jobs=[('dc_pp100x2_015',pdiode(100,0.15,2)),('dc_pp20_1',pdiode(20,1,1)),('dc_nn100x8_05',ndiode(100,0.5,8)),('dc_nn10_1',ndiode(10,1,1)),('dc_pp100_05',pdiode(100,0.5,1))]
    for pvt in ('N','FLC','SHH'):
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(5) as ex:
            res=list(ex.map(lambda j: (j[0],run(j[0],j[1],pvt)), jobs))
        for n,o in res: print(pvt,n,o)

Sweep mirror PMOS L and W at low-VIN corners

cd /workspace; cat > run4.py <<'EOF'
from corners import *
import time
base=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12)
variants=[dict(Wm=Wm,Lm=Lm) for Wm in (20,40) for Lm in (0.15,0.25,0.4)]
jobs=[]
for i,v in enumerate(variants):
    p=dict(base); p.update(v)
    for pvt in ('N','SLC','SLH','FLC','FLH'):
        jobs.append((f"V4_{i}_{pvt}", gen(p), pvt, 2e-10))
t=time.time(); res=par(jobs, workers=6); print('elapsed',round(time.time()-t))
for nm,pvt,cl,ev in sorted(res,key=lambda x:x[0]):
    i=int(nm.split('_')[1])
    print(i, variants[i], pvt, short(ev))

Probe node voltages at SLC and N for all three loads

from probe import *
from topoV import gen
p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12,Wm=20,Lm=0.25)

from corners import *
import time
base=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12,Wm=20,Lm=0.25)

    for pvt in ('N','SLC','FHH'):

Characterize NMOS Vgs vs geometry at three corners

cd /workspace; cat > devchar3.py <<'EOF'
from devchar import *
def ndio_vss(W,L,m,R=100e3): return f".subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF\nR1 VIN nb {R}\nXM nb nb VSS VSS {NF} W={W} L={L} nf=1 m={m}\n.ends DUT\n"
def f(j):
    n,net,o,pvt=j
    rep=run_one(n,net,pvt,2e-10,analysis='dc_low',exp=f"{n}_{pvt}")
    c=rep['cases'][0]['measurements']
    iin=c['signed_port_currents_a']['vin']
    vin={'SLC':1.15,'N':1.25,'FHH':1.35,'FLC':1.15,'SHH':1.35}[pvt]
    return pvt,o,iin,vin-iin*100e3
opts=[(100,0.15,8),(100,0.25,8),(100,0.5,8),(100,0.5,4),(100,1.0,4),(100,1.0,8),(100,2.0,4),(50,0.5,16),(10,0.5,32),(100,0.35,8)]
jobs=[(f"dn_{i}",ndio_vss(*o),o,pvt) for i,o in enumerate(opts) for pvt in ('SLC','N','FHH')]
with ThreadPoolExecutor(6) as ex:
    for pvt,o,iin,vgs in ex.map(f,jobs):
        print(pvt,'W,L,m=',o,'W/L=',round(o[0]*o[2]/o[1]),'Iin=%.2fuA'%(iin*1e6),'Vgs=%.3f'%vgs)
EOF

import time, sys
from lib import *
from topoV import gen
PVTS=['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
def corner_check(p, name, pvts=PVTS, cls=(2e-10,5e-10), netlist=None, workers=6):
    nl = netlist if netlist is not None else gen(p)
    jobs=[(f"{name}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}", nl, pvt, cl) for pvt in pvts for cl in cls]
    t=time.time(); res=par(jobs,workers=workers)
    tot=0; fails=0
    for nm,pvt,cl,ev in sorted(res,key=lambda x:(x[1],x[2])):
        print(pvt, int(cl*1e12), short(ev))
    print('elapsed',round(time.time()-t))
    return res
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12)
    corner_check(p,'C1')

Sweep larger input pair with smaller tail multiple

cat > multi.py <<'EOF'
import sys, time
from lib import *
from topoV import gen
BASE=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12,Wm=20,Lm=0.25)
def multi(plist, prefix, pvts=('SLC','SHC','FHH'), cl=2e-10, workers=6, gen=gen):
    jobs=[]; names={}
    for i,p in enumerate(plist):
        d=dict(BASE); d.update(p)
        for pvt in pvts:
            nm=f"{prefix}{i:02d}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}"
            jobs.append((nm,gen(d),pvt,cl)); names[nm]=(i,p)
    t=time.time(); res=par(jobs,workers=workers)
    byi={}
    for nm,pvt,cl_,ev in res:
        byi.setdefault(names[nm][0],[]).append((pvt,ev))
    for i in sorted(byi):
        print(i, plist[i])
        for pvt,ev in sorted(byi[i]):
            print('   ',pvt, short(ev))
    print('elapsed',round(time.time()-t))
    return res
EOF

Review probe helper usage

import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from lib import *
def probe(base, nodes, pvt='N', cl=2e-10, analysis='dc_low', R=10e6, tag='P', workers=6):
    def f(n):
        net = base.replace('.ends DUT', f'Rprobe {n} VREF {R}\n.ends DUT')
        name=f"{tag}_{n}_{pvt}"
        rep = run_one(name, net, pvt, cl, analysis=analysis, exp=f"{name}_{analysis}_{int(cl*1e12)}")
        out={}
        for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
            m=c['measurements']
            iv=m['signed_port_currents_a']['vref']
            out[c['case_id']]=(0.5-iv*R, m.get('vout_v'))
        return n,out
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(workers) as ex:
        return dict(ex.map(f,nodes))
if __name__=='__main__':
    from topoV import gen
    p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12)
    base=gen(p)
    for pvt in ('N','FLC','SLC','SHC'):
        r=probe(base,['g','na','nt','nb','fb'],pvt=pvt,tag='PR')
        print(pvt,{n:{k:round(v[0],3) for k,v in o.items()} for n,o in r.items()}, 'vout', {k:round(v[1],3) for k,v in r['g'].items()})
from probe import *
from topoV import gen
p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12,Wm=20,Lm=0.25)
base=gen(p)
for pvt in ('SLC','N'):
    r=probe(base,['g','na','nt','nb','fb'],pvt=pvt,analysis='dc',tag='PQ')
    for n,o in r.items():
        print(pvt,n,{k:round(v[0],3) for k,v in o.items()})
    print(pvt,'vout',{k:round(v[1],3) for k,v in r['g'].items()})

Probe internal node voltages for mi=16 Nt=6 design

cat > run11.py <<'EOF'
from probe import *
from topoV import gen
from multi import BASE
import json
def go(p,tag):
    d=dict(BASE); d.update(p); base=gen(d)
    for pvt,vin in (('SLC',1.15),('SHC',1.35),('N',1.25),('FHH',1.35)):
        r=probe(base,['g','na','nt','nb'],pvt=pvt,analysis='dc',tag=tag)
        out={}
        for n,o in r.items():
            out[n]={k:round(v[0],3) for k,v in o.items()}
        vout={k:round(v[1],3) for k,v in r['g'].items()}
        # tail current estimate
        Ib={k:(vin-out['nb'][k])/d['Rb']*1e6 for k in out['nb']}
        print(pvt,out,'vout',vout,'Ib(uA)',{k:round(v,1) for k,v in Ib.items()})
go(dict(mi=16,Nt=6,Cff=8e-12),'PW')

cat > topoY.py <<'EOF'
import topoV
DEF=dict(topoV.DEF); DEF.update(Cc=0.0)
def gen(p):
    d=dict(DEF); d.update(p)
    cc=d.pop('Cc')
    s=topoV.gen(d)
    if cc>0:
        s=s.replace('.ends DUT', f'Cc VOUT g {cc}\n.ends DUT')
    return s

cat > topoZ.py <<'EOF'
NF='sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8'; PF='sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8'
DEF=dict(Rb=80e3,Wb=10,Lb=1,Nt=6,Wi=100,Li=0.5,mi=8,Wm=20,Lm=0.25,mm=1,Wp=100,Lp=0.15,mp=4,Rd=60e3,gam=0.1,Rtot=1e6,Cff=4e-12,Rbl=100e3,Rf=60e3,Cx=15e-12,Cc=4e-12,Cr=0.0)
def gen(p):
    d=dict(DEF); d.update(p)
    g=d['gam']
    if g>0:
        R3=d['Rd']*(1-g)/(2*g); Ra=g*d['Rtot']; Rb=(1-g)*d['Rtot']
        mix=f"R3 VIN fb {R3}\nRra VREF rf {Ra}\nRrb rf VIN {Rb}\n"
        rg='rf'
    else:
        mix=""; rg='VREF'
    cr = f"Cr rf VSS {d['Cr']}\n" if (g>0 and d['Cr']>0) else ""
    cc = f"Cc VOUT g {d['Cc']}\n" if d['Cc']>0 else ""
    return f"""* z-family
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
Rb VIN nb {d['Rb']}
XMb nb nb VSS VSS {NF} W={d['Wb']} L={d['Lb']} nf=1 m=1
XMt nt nb VSS VSS {NF} W={d['Wb']} L={d['Lb']} nf=1 m={d['Nt']}
XM1 na fb nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m={d['mi']}
XM2 g {rg} nt VSS {NF} W={d['Wi']} L={d['Li']} nf=1 m={d['mi']}
XM3 na na VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wm']} L={d['Lm']} nf=1 m={d['mm']}
XM4 g na VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wm']} L={d['Lm']} nf=1 m={d['mm']}
XMp VOUT g VIN VIN {PF} W={d['Wp']} L={d['Lp']} nf=1 m={d['mp']}
R1 VOUT fb {d['Rd']}
R2 fb VSS {d['Rd']}
{mix}Cff VOUT fb {d['Cff']}
{cr}Rbl VOUT VSS {d['Rbl']}
Rf g gx {d['Rf']}
Cx gx VIN {d['Cx']}
{cc}.ends DUT
"""
EOF

Inspect droop vs overshoot split for best variant

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json,glob
for pvt in ('FHH','SLC'):
    f=glob.glob(f'runs/Z205_{pvt}_200/Z205_{pvt}_200_{pvt}_200.json')[0]
    d=json.load(open(f))
    for c in d['cases']:
        if c['case_id'] in ('light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst'):
            m=c['measurements']; e=m['envelope']
            print(pvt,c['case_id'],'env droop',round(e['droop_v'],4),'ovs',round(e['overshoot_v'],4),'min',round(e['min_v'],4),'max',round(e['max_v'],4))
            for w in m['recovery_windows']:
                print('    win droop',round(w['droop_v'],4),'ovs',round(w['overshoot_v'],4),'rec',w['recovery_s'])
EOF

Recall corner_check usage

sed -n 1,60p corners.py; ls run*.py | tail -5; tail -15 run20.py
RESULT
2:29:13
Bash
import time, sys
from lib import *
from topoV import gen
PVTS=['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
def corner_check(p, name, pvts=PVTS, cls=(2e-10,5e-10), netlist=None, workers=6):
    nl = netlist if netlist is not None else gen(p)
    jobs=[(f"{name}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e12)}", nl, pvt, cl) for pvt in pvts for cl in cls]
    t=time.time(); res=par(jobs,workers=workers)
    tot=0; fails=0
    for nm,pvt,cl,ev in sorted(res,key=lambda x:(x[1],x[2])):
        print(pvt, int(cl*1e12), short(ev))
    print('elapsed',round(time.time()-t))
    return res
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=dict(Wi=100,mi=8,Li=0.5,Nt=8,Cx=15e-12,Rf=30e3,mp=2,Wp=100,Cff=4e-12)
    corner_check(p,'C1')

# Shared client

`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

Submit the final G1 design with rationale

cd /workspace; atb-client status 2>&1 | grep -m1 remaining_seconds; atb-client submit --rationale "Single-stage NMOS-input OTA (resistor-biased tail mirror from VIN, long-L PMOS mirror load for DC gain) drives a wide PMOS pass device (400um/0.15um). Both OTA inputs are raised to a VIN-tracking common mode: the feedback divider (60k/60k) gets a VIN leg (R3) and the VREF side gets a matched Rra/Rrb mix to VIN, so VOUT = 2*VREF independent of VIN, the tail stays saturated across PVT and |Iref| <= 0.85 uA. Cr (4 pF) on the reference node removes Miller feedthrough from the pass gate. Rf (100k) + Cx (18 pF) to VIN gives a PI-like gate network, Cc (1 pF) VOUT-to-gate gives pole splitting and fast feed-forward for load steps, Cff (4 pF) across R1 compensates the feedback node, and a 70k bleeder keeps a minimum pass current. Total explicit C = 27 pF, MOS WL ~960 um2. Verified on all 9 PVT x both CL (200/500 pF): 20/20 required rows each; worst DC error 21 mV, worst transient envelope 53 mV, worst overhead 87.8 uA at 25 uA load (FHH), recovery <= 2 us." 2>&1 | head -30

MODEL
2:39:19

Submitted revision dut-141cc36c02050896c2e85427. It passed all 20 required rows in every one of the 18 PVT × CL runs (9 corners, 200 pF and 500 pF). Submission returns no verification feedback, so the results below are my own measurements.

The final circuit is an NMOS-input OTA driving a wide PMOS pass device, with both OTA inputs raised to a common mode that tracks VIN. That makes VOUT equal 2·VREF regardless of VIN and keeps the tail current source saturated across corners. The reference chain draws at most 0.85 µA from VREF. The remaining compensation parts are a gate network to VIN, a 1 pF feed-forward capacitor from VOUT to the pass gate, a 4 pF capacitor across R1 and a 70k bleeder.

Worst-case figures against the spec:

Check Worst case measured Limit
DC error 21.3 mV (FLC) 30 mV
Transient envelope 53 mV (SLH) 80 mV
Recovery about 1.8 µs 5 µs
Overhead at 25 µA load 87.8 µA (FHH) 100 µA
Total explicit capacitance 27 pF 30 pF
MOS ΣW·L 960 µm² 10000 µm²

Removing the 1 pF feed-forward capacitor makes SHC at 200 pF fail, so that capacitor has to stay.

The client says the final evaluation uses independent conditions I cannot see. I assumed they match the public 9 × 2 grid, so the margins above only hold if they do.
